- **create-product**: Create a new product package and take it through the content SDLC. A catalog product (the default) goes scaffold → gradle gate → PR to dev. USE THIS when the user says "add product X", "register &lt;vendor&gt;'s &lt;tool&gt; as a product", "make product X", or a ZeroBias task asks for a product package. Standalone: works in this repo alone (no meta-repo), and no platform task is required (task-driven mode is optional).
